### 词群 17.01｜规则以什么形式存在 {#group-17-01}

**串联方式：** 从法律、规章和条款进入条约、协议、宪章与议定书，再用规范、基准和标准衡量。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">law<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-001" aria-label="定位 law">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-001" data-mark-label="law" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="law：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="law：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=law&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 law" title="在有道查询 law">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 法律；规律；定理</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each item in the safety regulation reflects a legal rule established by <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=law&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>law</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">安全法规中的每一条都体现了法律确立的规则。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The committee reviewed an item of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=law&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>law</strong></a> to clarify the legal basis of the new rule and regulation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">委员会审查了一项法律条文，澄清新规则与法规的法律依据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Legal advisers checked every item to ensure that the school rule complied with the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=law&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>law</strong></a> and the relevant regulation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法律顾问逐项检查，确保校规符合法律和相关法规。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">criminal law</span> · 刑法</p>

</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-006">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-006" class="vocabulary-word-title">treaty<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-006" aria-label="定位 treaty">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-006" data-mark-label="treaty" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="treaty：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="treaty：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=treaty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 treaty" title="在有道查询 treaty">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-006</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 条约；协议</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=treaty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>treaty</strong></a> created a shared charter, while a later agreement added a protocol on emissions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">条约确立了共同宪章，随后一项协议增加了排放议定书。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Delegates compared the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=treaty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>treaty</strong></a> with the organisation's charter before approving the agreement and its protocol.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">代表们将条约与该组织宪章比较后，批准了协议及其议定书。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The charter requires any new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=treaty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>treaty</strong></a> or protocol to receive approval under the existing agreement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">宪章要求所有新条约或议定书依照现有协议获得批准。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">sign a treaty</span> · 签署条约</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diplomacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">diplomacy</a></span> · 外交</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ratification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ratification</a></span> · 批准</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-007">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-007" class="vocabulary-word-title">agreement<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-007" aria-label="定位 agreement">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-007" data-mark-label="agreement" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="agreement：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="agreement：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=agreement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 agreement" title="在有道查询 agreement">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-007</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 协定；同意</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The treaty created a shared charter, while a later <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=agreement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>agreement</strong></a> added a protocol on emissions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">条约确立了共同宪章，随后一项协议增加了排放议定书。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Delegates compared the treaty with the organisation's charter before approving the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=agreement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>agreement</strong></a> and its protocol.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">代表们将条约与该组织宪章比较后，批准了协议及其议定书。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The charter requires any new treaty or protocol to receive approval under the existing <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=agreement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>agreement</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">宪章要求所有新条约或议定书依照现有协议获得批准。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reach an agreement</span> · 达成协议</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diplomacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">diplomacy</a></span> · 外交</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ratification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ratification</a></span> · 批准</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-008">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-008" class="vocabulary-word-title">charter<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-008" aria-label="定位 charter">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-008" data-mark-label="charter" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="charter：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="charter：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=charter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 charter" title="在有道查询 charter">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-008</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 宪章；v. 租，包(船、军、飞机等)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The treaty created a shared <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=charter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>charter</strong></a>, while a later agreement added a protocol on emissions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">条约确立了共同宪章，随后一项协议增加了排放议定书。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Delegates compared the treaty with the organisation's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=charter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>charter</strong></a> before approving the agreement and its protocol.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">代表们将条约与该组织宪章比较后，批准了协议及其议定书。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=charter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>charter</strong></a> requires any new treaty or protocol to receive approval under the existing agreement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">宪章要求所有新条约或议定书依照现有协议获得批准。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a founding charter</span> · 创立宪章</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diplomacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">diplomacy</a></span> · 外交</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ratification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ratification</a></span> · 批准</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-009">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-009" class="vocabulary-word-title">protocol<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-009" aria-label="定位 protocol">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-009" data-mark-label="protocol" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="protocol：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="protocol：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protocol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 protocol" title="在有道查询 protocol">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-009</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 议定书，协议；礼仪</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The treaty created a shared charter, while a later agreement added a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protocol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>protocol</strong></a> on emissions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">条约确立了共同宪章，随后一项协议增加了排放议定书。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Delegates compared the treaty with the organisation's charter before approving the agreement and its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protocol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>protocol</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">代表们将条约与该组织宪章比较后，批准了协议及其议定书。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The charter requires any new treaty or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protocol&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>protocol</strong></a> to receive approval under the existing agreement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">宪章要求所有新条约或议定书依照现有协议获得批准。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an international protocol</span> · 国际议定书</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diplomacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">diplomacy</a></span> · 外交</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ratification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ratification</a></span> · 批准</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-010">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-010" class="vocabulary-word-title">norm<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-010" aria-label="定位 norm">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-010" data-mark-label="norm" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="norm：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="norm：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=norm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 norm" title="在有道查询 norm">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-010</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 标准；［～s］行为准则，规范</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The criteria use a national benchmark to define a minimum standard rather than a social <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=norm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>norm</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这些标准采用全国基准来界定最低水准，而非社会规范。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A social <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=norm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>norm</strong></a> may influence the criteria, but the benchmark must reflect an objective standard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">社会规范可能影响评判标准，但基准必须体现客观水准。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The panel revised its criteria after finding that the usual <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=norm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>norm</strong></a> fell below the benchmark for an acceptable standard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">评审组发现惯常规范低于合格水准的基准后，修订了标准。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a social norm</span> · 社会规范</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assessment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assessment</a></span> · 评估</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=measurement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">measurement</a></span> · 测量</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-011">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-011" class="vocabulary-word-title">benchmark<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-011" aria-label="定位 benchmark">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-011" data-mark-label="benchmark" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="benchmark：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="benchmark：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=benchmark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 benchmark" title="在有道查询 benchmark">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-011</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 基准，水准点</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The criteria use a national <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=benchmark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>benchmark</strong></a> to define a minimum standard rather than a social norm.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这些标准采用全国基准来界定最低水准，而非社会规范。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A social norm may influence the criteria, but the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=benchmark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>benchmark</strong></a> must reflect an objective standard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">社会规范可能影响评判标准，但基准必须体现客观水准。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The panel revised its criteria after finding that the usual norm fell below the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=benchmark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>benchmark</strong></a> for an acceptable standard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">评审组发现惯常规范低于合格水准的基准后，修订了标准。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a performance benchmark</span> · 绩效基准</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assessment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assessment</a></span> · 评估</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=measurement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">measurement</a></span> · 测量</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-012">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-012" class="vocabulary-word-title">criteria<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-012" aria-label="定位 criteria">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-012" data-mark-label="criteria" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="criteria：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="criteria：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=criteria&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 criteria" title="在有道查询 criteria">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-012</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. [criterion 的复数形式］标准，准则</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=criteria&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>criteria</strong></a> use a national benchmark to define a minimum standard rather than a social norm.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这些标准采用全国基准来界定最低水准，而非社会规范。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A social norm may influence the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=criteria&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>criteria</strong></a>, but the benchmark must reflect an objective standard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">社会规范可能影响评判标准，但基准必须体现客观水准。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The panel revised its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=criteria&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>criteria</strong></a> after finding that the usual norm fell below the benchmark for an acceptable standard.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">评审组发现惯常规范低于合格水准的基准后，修订了标准。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">selection criteria</span> · 选拔标准</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assessment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assessment</a></span> · 评估</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=measurement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">measurement</a></span> · 测量</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-013">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-013" class="vocabulary-word-title">standard<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-013" aria-label="定位 standard">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-013" data-mark-label="standard" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="standard：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="standard：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=standard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 standard" title="在有道查询 standard">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-013</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 标准</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The criteria use a national benchmark to define a minimum <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=standard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>standard</strong></a> rather than a social norm.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这些标准采用全国基准来界定最低水准，而非社会规范。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A social norm may influence the criteria, but the benchmark must reflect an objective <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=standard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>standard</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">社会规范可能影响评判标准，但基准必须体现客观水准。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The panel revised its criteria after finding that the usual norm fell below the benchmark for an acceptable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=standard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>standard</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">评审组发现惯常规范低于合格水准的基准后，修订了标准。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a minimum standard</span> · 最低标准</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assessment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assessment</a></span> · 评估</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=measurement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">measurement</a></span> · 测量</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 17.02｜法律怎样许可、约束与执行 {#group-17-02}

**串联方式：** 先看允许和准入，再看执行、违约、拒绝、强制、约束与义务。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-014">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-014" class="vocabulary-word-title">permit<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-014" aria-label="定位 permit">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-014" data-mark-label="permit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="permit：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="permit：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=permit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 permit" title="在有道查询 permit">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-014</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 允许，准许；n. 许可证</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=permit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>permit</strong></a> will allow admission to the research centre.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该许可证将允许持有人进入研究中心。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Staff only allow admission when visitors show a valid <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=permit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>permit</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工作人员只允许出示有效许可证的访客进入。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=permit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>permit</strong></a> does not allow free admission to special exhibitions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">此许可证不允许免费进入特别展览。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a parking permit</span> · 停车许可证</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=entry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">entry</a></span> · 进入</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=authorisation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">authorisation</a></span> · 授权</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-015">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-015" class="vocabulary-word-title">allow<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-015" aria-label="定位 allow">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-015" data-mark-label="allow" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="allow：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="allow：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 allow" title="在有道查询 allow">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-015</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 允许，准许</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The permit will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>allow</strong></a> admission to the research centre.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该许可证将允许持有人进入研究中心。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Staff only <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>allow</strong></a> admission when visitors show a valid permit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工作人员只允许出示有效许可证的访客进入。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This permit does not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>allow</strong></a> free admission to special exhibitions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">此许可证不允许免费进入特别展览。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">allow entry</span> · 允许进入</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=entry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">entry</a></span> · 进入</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=authorisation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">authorisation</a></span> · 授权</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-016">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-016" class="vocabulary-word-title">admission<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-016" aria-label="定位 admission">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-016" data-mark-label="admission" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="admission：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="admission：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=admission&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 admission" title="在有道查询 admission">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-016</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 承认；入场费；准许进入</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The permit will allow <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=admission&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>admission</strong></a> to the research centre.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该许可证将允许持有人进入研究中心。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Staff only allow <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=admission&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>admission</strong></a> when visitors show a valid permit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工作人员只允许出示有效许可证的访客进入。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This permit does not allow free <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=admission&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>admission</strong></a> to special exhibitions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">此许可证不允许免费进入特别展览。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">admission to a museum</span> · 博物馆入场</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=entry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">entry</a></span> · 进入</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=authorisation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">authorisation</a></span> · 授权</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-017">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-017" class="vocabulary-word-title">perform<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-017" aria-label="定位 perform">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-017" data-mark-label="perform" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="perform：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="perform：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perform&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 perform" title="在有道查询 perform">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-017</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 履行，执行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Banks may reject borrowers who default, while courts enforce lenders' duty to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perform&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perform</strong></a> contractual obligations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">银行可能拒绝违约借款人，而法院强制贷款方履行合同义务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agency must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perform&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perform</strong></a> checks, reject unsuitable applications and enforce repayment terms when borrowers default.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机构必须进行检查、拒绝不合适的申请，并在借款人违约时执行还款条款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If contractors default and reject requests to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perform&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perform</strong></a> repairs, the council can enforce the agreement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果承包商违约并拒绝维修要求，市政机构可以强制执行协议。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">perform a duty</span> · 履行职责</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contract&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contract</a></span> · 合同</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repayment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repayment</a></span> · 还款</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-018">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-018" class="vocabulary-word-title">enforce<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-018" aria-label="定位 enforce">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-018" data-mark-label="enforce" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="enforce：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="enforce：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enforce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 enforce" title="在有道查询 enforce">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-018</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 强制执行，强行实施；迫使</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Banks may reject borrowers who default, while courts <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enforce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enforce</strong></a> lenders' duty to perform contractual obligations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">银行可能拒绝违约借款人，而法院强制贷款方履行合同义务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agency must perform checks, reject unsuitable applications and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enforce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enforce</strong></a> repayment terms when borrowers default.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机构必须进行检查、拒绝不合适的申请，并在借款人违约时执行还款条款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If contractors default and reject requests to perform repairs, the council can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enforce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enforce</strong></a> the agreement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果承包商违约并拒绝维修要求，市政机构可以强制执行协议。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">enforce a contract</span> · 执行合同</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contract&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contract</a></span> · 合同</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repayment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repayment</a></span> · 还款</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-019">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-019" class="vocabulary-word-title">default<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-019" aria-label="定位 default">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-019" data-mark-label="default" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="default：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="default：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=default&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 default" title="在有道查询 default">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-019</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 不支付，不履行；n. 违约；默认值</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Banks may reject borrowers who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=default&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>default</strong></a>, while courts enforce lenders' duty to perform contractual obligations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">银行可能拒绝违约借款人，而法院强制贷款方履行合同义务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agency must perform checks, reject unsuitable applications and enforce repayment terms when borrowers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=default&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>default</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机构必须进行检查、拒绝不合适的申请，并在借款人违约时执行还款条款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If contractors <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=default&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>default</strong></a> and reject requests to perform repairs, the council can enforce the agreement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果承包商违约并拒绝维修要求，市政机构可以强制执行协议。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">default on a loan</span> · 拖欠贷款</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contract&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contract</a></span> · 合同</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repayment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repayment</a></span> · 还款</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-020">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-020" class="vocabulary-word-title">reject<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-020" aria-label="定位 reject">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-020" data-mark-label="reject" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reject：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reject：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reject&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reject" title="在有道查询 reject">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-020</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.拒绝接受；摒弃</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Banks may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reject&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reject</strong></a> borrowers who default, while courts enforce lenders' duty to perform contractual obligations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">银行可能拒绝违约借款人，而法院强制贷款方履行合同义务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agency must perform checks, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reject&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reject</strong></a> unsuitable applications and enforce repayment terms when borrowers default.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机构必须进行检查、拒绝不合适的申请，并在借款人违约时执行还款条款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If contractors default and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reject&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reject</strong></a> requests to perform repairs, the council can enforce the agreement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果承包商违约并拒绝维修要求，市政机构可以强制执行协议。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reject an application</span> · 拒绝申请</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contract&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contract</a></span> · 合同</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repayment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repayment</a></span> · 还款</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-021">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-021" class="vocabulary-word-title">constraint<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-021" aria-label="定位 constraint">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-021" data-mark-label="constraint" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="constraint：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="constraint：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=constraint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 constraint" title="在有道查询 constraint">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-021</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 约束，限制；克制</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The official notice made training mandatory, creating a potent incentive to overcome the budget <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=constraint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>constraint</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">正式通知将培训列为强制要求，形成了克服预算限制的有力激励。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Despite a financial <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=constraint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>constraint</strong></a>, the official programme retained mandatory inspections as a potent safety measure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">尽管财务受限，官方项目仍保留强制检查，作为有力的安全措施。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Mandatory reporting can be a potent <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=constraint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>constraint</strong></a> on misconduct when supported by official oversight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在官方监督支持下，强制报告能有效约束不当行为。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a budget constraint</span> · 预算限制</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accountability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accountability</a></span> · 问责</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inspection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inspection</a></span> · 检查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-022">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-022" class="vocabulary-word-title">official<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-022" aria-label="定位 official">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-022" data-mark-label="official" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="official：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="official：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=official&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 official" title="在有道查询 official">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-022</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 正式的，官方的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=official&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>official</strong></a> notice made training mandatory, creating a potent incentive to overcome the budget constraint.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">正式通知将培训列为强制要求，形成了克服预算限制的有力激励。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Despite a financial constraint, the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=official&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>official</strong></a> programme retained mandatory inspections as a potent safety measure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">尽管财务受限，官方项目仍保留强制检查，作为有力的安全措施。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Mandatory reporting can be a potent constraint on misconduct when supported by <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=official&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>official</strong></a> oversight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在官方监督支持下，强制报告能有效约束不当行为。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an official notice</span> · 正式通知</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accountability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accountability</a></span> · 问责</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inspection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inspection</a></span> · 检查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Despite a financial constraint, the official programme retained <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mandatory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mandatory</strong></a> inspections as a potent safety measure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">尽管财务受限，官方项目仍保留强制检查，作为有力的安全措施。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mandatory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Mandatory</strong></a> reporting can be a potent constraint on misconduct when supported by official oversight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在官方监督支持下，强制报告能有效约束不当行为。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Despite a financial constraint, the official programme retained mandatory inspections as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=potent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>potent</strong></a> safety measure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">尽管财务受限，官方项目仍保留强制检查，作为有力的安全措施。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-025">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-025" class="vocabulary-word-title">bound<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-025" aria-label="定位 bound">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-025" data-mark-label="bound" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bound：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bound：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bound&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bound" title="在有道查询 bound">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-025</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. [be ~ to］一定会，很可能会，有义务</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Employers are <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bound&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bound</strong></a> by their obligation to provide a safe workplace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雇主受到提供安全工作场所这一义务的约束。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agreement leaves both parties <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bound&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bound</strong></a> by an obligation of confidentiality.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">协议使双方都承担保密义务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Even volunteers may be <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bound&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bound</strong></a> by an obligation to protect personal data.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">即使志愿者也可能负有保护个人数据的义务。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">bound by law</span> · 受法律约束</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=duty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">duty</a></span> · 责任</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=confidentiality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">confidentiality</a></span> · 保密</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-026">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-026" class="vocabulary-word-title">obligation<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-026" aria-label="定位 obligation">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-026" data-mark-label="obligation" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="obligation：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="obligation：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obligation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 obligation" title="在有道查询 obligation">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-026</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 义务，责任</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Employers are bound by their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obligation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>obligation</strong></a> to provide a safe workplace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">雇主受到提供安全工作场所这一义务的约束。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agreement leaves both parties bound by an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obligation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>obligation</strong></a> of confidentiality.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">协议使双方都承担保密义务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Even volunteers may be bound by an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obligation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>obligation</strong></a> to protect personal data.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">即使志愿者也可能负有保护个人数据的义务。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a legal obligation</span> · 法律义务</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=duty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">duty</a></span> · 责任</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=confidentiality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">confidentiality</a></span> · 保密</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 17.03｜谁立法，如何评价公正 {#group-17-03}

**串联方式：** 从法院和立法进入公平、公正、偏见与歧视，分清制度与评价。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-027">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-027" class="vocabulary-word-title">court<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-027" aria-label="定位 court">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-027" data-mark-label="court" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="court：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="court：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=court&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 court" title="在有道查询 court">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-027</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.法院；庭院</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The supreme <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=court&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>court</strong></a> explained how parliament could legislate and enact a revised law.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">最高法院解释了议会如何立法并通过修订后的法律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Lawmakers plan to legislate and enact protections after a supreme <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=court&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>court</strong></a> ruling exposed a gap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">最高法院裁决暴露法律空缺后，立法者计划制定并通过保护措施。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they enact the bill, legislators must consider whether the supreme <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=court&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>court</strong></a> permits them to legislate in this area.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通过法案前，立法者必须考虑最高法院是否允许他们在此领域立法。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a civil court</span> · 民事法院</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parliament&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">parliament</a></span> · 议会</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=constitution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">constitution</a></span> · 宪法</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-028">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-028" class="vocabulary-word-title">supreme<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-028" aria-label="定位 supreme">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-028" data-mark-label="supreme" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="supreme：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="supreme：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=supreme&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 supreme" title="在有道查询 supreme">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-028</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 最高的；最大的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=supreme&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>supreme</strong></a> court explained how parliament could legislate and enact a revised law.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">最高法院解释了议会如何立法并通过修订后的法律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Lawmakers plan to legislate and enact protections after a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=supreme&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>supreme</strong></a> court ruling exposed a gap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">最高法院裁决暴露法律空缺后，立法者计划制定并通过保护措施。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they enact the bill, legislators must consider whether the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=supreme&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>supreme</strong></a> court permits them to legislate in this area.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通过法案前，立法者必须考虑最高法院是否允许他们在此领域立法。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the supreme court</span> · 最高法院</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parliament&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">parliament</a></span> · 议会</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=constitution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">constitution</a></span> · 宪法</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-029">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-029" class="vocabulary-word-title">enact<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-029" aria-label="定位 enact">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-029" data-mark-label="enact" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="enact：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="enact：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enact&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 enact" title="在有道查询 enact">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-029</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 通过(法律)；表演</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The supreme court explained how parliament could legislate and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enact&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enact</strong></a> a revised law.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">最高法院解释了议会如何立法并通过修订后的法律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Lawmakers plan to legislate and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enact&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enact</strong></a> protections after a supreme court ruling exposed a gap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">最高法院裁决暴露法律空缺后，立法者计划制定并通过保护措施。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enact&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enact</strong></a> the bill, legislators must consider whether the supreme court permits them to legislate in this area.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通过法案前，立法者必须考虑最高法院是否允许他们在此领域立法。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">enact legislation</span> · 通过立法</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parliament&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">parliament</a></span> · 议会</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=constitution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">constitution</a></span> · 宪法</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-030">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-030" class="vocabulary-word-title">legislate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-030" aria-label="定位 legislate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-030" data-mark-label="legislate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="legislate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="legislate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=legislate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 legislate" title="在有道查询 legislate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-030</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 立法，制定(或通过)法律</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The supreme court explained how parliament could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=legislate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>legislate</strong></a> and enact a revised law.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">最高法院解释了议会如何立法并通过修订后的法律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Lawmakers plan to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=legislate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>legislate</strong></a> and enact protections after a supreme court ruling exposed a gap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">最高法院裁决暴露法律空缺后，立法者计划制定并通过保护措施。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they enact the bill, legislators must consider whether the supreme court permits them to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=legislate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>legislate</strong></a> in this area.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通过法案前，立法者必须考虑最高法院是否允许他们在此领域立法。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">legislate against discrimination</span> · 立法反对歧视</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parliament&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">parliament</a></span> · 议会</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=constitution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">constitution</a></span> · 宪法</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-031">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-031" class="vocabulary-word-title">equity<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-031" aria-label="定位 equity">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-031" data-mark-label="equity" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="equity：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="equity：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 equity" title="在有道查询 equity">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-031</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 公平</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A just society promotes <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>equity</strong></a> through fair policies that challenge prejudice and discrimination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公正社会通过挑战偏见和歧视的公平政策促进平等。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Equity</strong></a> requires a fair hearing and a just decision free from prejudice and discrimination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公平需要不受偏见和歧视影响的公正听证与判决。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The campaign links <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>equity</strong></a> to a just workplace where fair recruitment reduces prejudice and discrimination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">运动把公平与公正职场联系起来，在那里公平招聘能减少偏见和歧视。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">equity in education</span> · 教育公平</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equal%20opportunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">equal opportunity</a></span> · 平等机会</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inclusion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inclusion</a></span> · 包容</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-032">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-032" class="vocabulary-word-title">fair<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-032" aria-label="定位 fair">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-032" data-mark-label="fair" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fair：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fair：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fair" title="在有道查询 fair">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-032</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 公平的，公正的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A just society promotes equity through <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fair</strong></a> policies that challenge prejudice and discrimination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公正社会通过挑战偏见和歧视的公平政策促进平等。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Equity requires a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fair</strong></a> hearing and a just decision free from prejudice and discrimination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公平需要不受偏见和歧视影响的公正听证与判决。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The campaign links equity to a just workplace where <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fair</strong></a> recruitment reduces prejudice and discrimination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">运动把公平与公正职场联系起来，在那里公平招聘能减少偏见和歧视。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a fair hearing</span> · 公正听证</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equal%20opportunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">equal opportunity</a></span> · 平等机会</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inclusion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inclusion</a></span> · 包容</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-033">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-033" class="vocabulary-word-title">just<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-033" aria-label="定位 just">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-033" data-mark-label="just" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="just：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="just：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=just&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 just" title="在有道查询 just">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-033</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 公正的；正义的；adv. 刚刚；仅仅</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=just&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>just</strong></a> society promotes equity through fair policies that challenge prejudice and discrimination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公正社会通过挑战偏见和歧视的公平政策促进平等。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Equity requires a fair hearing and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=just&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>just</strong></a> decision free from prejudice and discrimination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公平需要不受偏见和歧视影响的公正听证与判决。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The campaign links equity to a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=just&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>just</strong></a> workplace where fair recruitment reduces prejudice and discrimination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">运动把公平与公正职场联系起来，在那里公平招聘能减少偏见和歧视。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a just society</span> · 公正社会</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equal%20opportunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">equal opportunity</a></span> · 平等机会</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inclusion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inclusion</a></span> · 包容</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-034">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-034" class="vocabulary-word-title">prejudice<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-034" aria-label="定位 prejudice">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-034" data-mark-label="prejudice" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="prejudice：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="prejudice：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prejudice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 prejudice" title="在有道查询 prejudice">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-034</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 偏见，成见</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A just society promotes equity through fair policies that challenge <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prejudice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prejudice</strong></a> and discrimination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公正社会通过挑战偏见和歧视的公平政策促进平等。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Equity requires a fair hearing and a just decision free from <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prejudice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prejudice</strong></a> and discrimination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公平需要不受偏见和歧视影响的公正听证与判决。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The campaign links equity to a just workplace where fair recruitment reduces <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prejudice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prejudice</strong></a> and discrimination.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">运动把公平与公正职场联系起来，在那里公平招聘能减少偏见和歧视。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">racial prejudice</span> · 种族偏见</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equal%20opportunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">equal opportunity</a></span> · 平等机会</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inclusion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inclusion</a></span> · 包容</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-035">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-035" class="vocabulary-word-title">discrimination<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-035" aria-label="定位 discrimination">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-035" data-mark-label="discrimination" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="discrimination：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="discrimination：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discrimination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 discrimination" title="在有道查询 discrimination">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-035</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 歧视</p>
