<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A just society promotes equity through fair policies that challenge prejudice and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discrimination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>discrimination</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公正社会通过挑战偏见和歧视的公平政策促进平等。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Equity requires a fair hearing and a just decision free from prejudice and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discrimination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>discrimination</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公平需要不受偏见和歧视影响的公正听证与判决。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The campaign links equity to a just workplace where fair recruitment reduces prejudice and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=discrimination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>discrimination</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">运动把公平与公正职场联系起来，在那里公平招聘能减少偏见和歧视。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">gender discrimination</span> · 性别歧视</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=equal%20opportunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">equal opportunity</a></span> · 平等机会</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inclusion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inclusion</a></span> · 包容</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 17.04｜争议怎样进入司法或调解 {#group-17-04}

**串联方式：** 从指控、起诉和谴责进入申诉、抵制、开庭、调解、答辩和上诉。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-036">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-036" class="vocabulary-word-title">accuse<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-036" aria-label="定位 accuse">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-036" data-mark-label="accuse" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="accuse：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="accuse：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 accuse" title="在有道查询 accuse">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-036</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.控告，指责</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Victims may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accuse</strong></a> the company of negligence and sue for damages, while authorities decide whether to prosecute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">受害者可指控公司疏忽并起诉索赔，由当局决定是否提起公诉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accuse</strong></a> someone is not necessarily to sue them or persuade the state to prosecute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">指控某人不一定意味着对其起诉，也不意味着说服国家提起公诉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report prompted residents to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accuse</strong></a> the operator of misconduct, sue the firm and ask regulators to prosecute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告促使居民指控运营商违规、起诉公司，并要求监管机构提起公诉。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">accuse someone of theft</span> · 指控某人盗窃</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defendant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">defendant</a></span> · 被告</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=damages&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">damages</a></span> · 损害赔偿</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-037">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-037" class="vocabulary-word-title">sue<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-037" aria-label="定位 sue">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-037" data-mark-label="sue" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sue：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sue：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sue" title="在有道查询 sue">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-037</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 控告，提起诉讼；提出请求</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Victims may accuse the company of negligence and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sue</strong></a> for damages, while authorities decide whether to prosecute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">受害者可指控公司疏忽并起诉索赔，由当局决定是否提起公诉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To accuse someone is not necessarily to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sue</strong></a> them or persuade the state to prosecute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">指控某人不一定意味着对其起诉，也不意味着说服国家提起公诉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report prompted residents to accuse the operator of misconduct, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sue</strong></a> the firm and ask regulators to prosecute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告促使居民指控运营商违规、起诉公司，并要求监管机构提起公诉。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">sue for damages</span> · 起诉索赔</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defendant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">defendant</a></span> · 被告</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=damages&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">damages</a></span> · 损害赔偿</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-038">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-038" class="vocabulary-word-title">prosecute<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-038" aria-label="定位 prosecute">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-038" data-mark-label="prosecute" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="prosecute：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="prosecute：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prosecute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 prosecute" title="在有道查询 prosecute">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-038</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.检举，起诉</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Victims may accuse the company of negligence and sue for damages, while authorities decide whether to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prosecute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prosecute</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">受害者可指控公司疏忽并起诉索赔，由当局决定是否提起公诉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To accuse someone is not necessarily to sue them or persuade the state to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prosecute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prosecute</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">指控某人不一定意味着对其起诉，也不意味着说服国家提起公诉。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report prompted residents to accuse the operator of misconduct, sue the firm and ask regulators to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prosecute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prosecute</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告促使居民指控运营商违规、起诉公司，并要求监管机构提起公诉。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">prosecute an offender</span> · 起诉违法者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=defendant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">defendant</a></span> · 被告</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=damages&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">damages</a></span> · 损害赔偿</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-039">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-039" class="vocabulary-word-title">condemn<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-039" aria-label="定位 condemn">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-039" data-mark-label="condemn" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="condemn：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="condemn：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=condemn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 condemn" title="在有道查询 condemn">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-039</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 谴责，指责</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Consumers may complain, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=condemn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>condemn</strong></a> unfair practices and boycott the brand.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">消费者可能投诉、谴责不公平做法，并抵制该品牌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers decided to complain formally rather than simply <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=condemn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>condemn</strong></a> the company or boycott its products.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人决定正式投诉，而非只是谴责公司或抵制其产品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Campaigners <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=condemn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>condemn</strong></a> pollution, complain to regulators and encourage people to boycott irresponsible firms.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">活动人士谴责污染，向监管者投诉，并鼓励公众抵制不负责任的企业。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">condemn violence</span> · 谴责暴力</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consumer%20action&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consumer action</a></span> · 消费者行动</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protest</a></span> · 抗议</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-040">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-040" class="vocabulary-word-title">complain<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-040" aria-label="定位 complain">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-040" data-mark-label="complain" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="complain：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="complain：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=complain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 complain" title="在有道查询 complain">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-040</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.抱怨；投诉</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Consumers may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=complain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>complain</strong></a>, condemn unfair practices and boycott the brand.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">消费者可能投诉、谴责不公平做法，并抵制该品牌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers decided to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=complain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>complain</strong></a> formally rather than simply condemn the company or boycott its products.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人决定正式投诉，而非只是谴责公司或抵制其产品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Campaigners condemn pollution, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=complain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>complain</strong></a> to regulators and encourage people to boycott irresponsible firms.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">活动人士谴责污染，向监管者投诉，并鼓励公众抵制不负责任的企业。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">complain about service</span> · 投诉服务</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consumer%20action&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consumer action</a></span> · 消费者行动</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protest</a></span> · 抗议</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-041">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-041" class="vocabulary-word-title">boycott<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-041" aria-label="定位 boycott">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-041" data-mark-label="boycott" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="boycott：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="boycott：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boycott&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 boycott" title="在有道查询 boycott">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-041</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 抵制，拒绝购买；n.抵制行动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Consumers may complain, condemn unfair practices and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boycott&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>boycott</strong></a> the brand.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">消费者可能投诉、谴责不公平做法，并抵制该品牌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers decided to complain formally rather than simply condemn the company or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boycott&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>boycott</strong></a> its products.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人决定正式投诉，而非只是谴责公司或抵制其产品。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Campaigners condemn pollution, complain to regulators and encourage people to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boycott&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>boycott</strong></a> irresponsible firms.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">活动人士谴责污染，向监管者投诉，并鼓励公众抵制不负责任的企业。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">boycott a brand</span> · 抵制品牌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consumer%20action&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consumer action</a></span> · 消费者行动</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protest</a></span> · 抗议</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-042">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-042" class="vocabulary-word-title">session<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-042" aria-label="定位 session">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-042" data-mark-label="session" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="session：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="session：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=session&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 session" title="在有道查询 session">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-042</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 会议；会期</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=session&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>session</strong></a>, lawyers tried to mediate after a plea for compromise and before the appeal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">会上，律师在有人请求妥协后、上诉之前尝试调解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The judge heard a plea to mediate the dispute during a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=session&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>session</strong></a> devoted to the appeal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法官在专门处理上诉的庭审中听取了调解争端的请求。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A final <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=session&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>session</strong></a> may mediate a settlement if both parties accept the plea to suspend the appeal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果双方接受暂停上诉的请求，最后一次会议可能促成调解和解。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a mediation session</span> · 调解会议</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=settlement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">settlement</a></span> · 和解</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compromise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compromise</a></span> · 妥协</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-043">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-043" class="vocabulary-word-title">mediate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-043" aria-label="定位 mediate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-043" data-mark-label="mediate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mediate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mediate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mediate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mediate" title="在有道查询 mediate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-043</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 斡旋，调解</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the session, lawyers tried to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mediate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mediate</strong></a> after a plea for compromise and before the appeal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">会上，律师在有人请求妥协后、上诉之前尝试调解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The judge heard a plea to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mediate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mediate</strong></a> the dispute during a session devoted to the appeal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法官在专门处理上诉的庭审中听取了调解争端的请求。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A final session may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mediate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mediate</strong></a> a settlement if both parties accept the plea to suspend the appeal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果双方接受暂停上诉的请求，最后一次会议可能促成调解和解。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">mediate a dispute</span> · 调解争端</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=settlement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">settlement</a></span> · 和解</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compromise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compromise</a></span> · 妥协</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-044">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-044" class="vocabulary-word-title">plea<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-044" aria-label="定位 plea">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-044" data-mark-label="plea" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="plea：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="plea：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plea&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 plea" title="在有道查询 plea">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-044</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 辩解理由，借口；请求</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the session, lawyers tried to mediate after a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plea&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plea</strong></a> for compromise and before the appeal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">会上，律师在有人请求妥协后、上诉之前尝试调解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The judge heard a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plea&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plea</strong></a> to mediate the dispute during a session devoted to the appeal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法官在专门处理上诉的庭审中听取了调解争端的请求。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A final session may mediate a settlement if both parties accept the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=plea&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>plea</strong></a> to suspend the appeal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果双方接受暂停上诉的请求，最后一次会议可能促成调解和解。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">make a plea</span> · 提出请求</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=settlement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">settlement</a></span> · 和解</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compromise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compromise</a></span> · 妥协</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-045">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-045" class="vocabulary-word-title">appeal<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-045" aria-label="定位 appeal">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-045" data-mark-label="appeal" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="appeal：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="appeal：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appeal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 appeal" title="在有道查询 appeal">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-045</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.呼吁；申诉；n. 呼吁；申诉</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the session, lawyers tried to mediate after a plea for compromise and before the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appeal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>appeal</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">会上，律师在有人请求妥协后、上诉之前尝试调解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The judge heard a plea to mediate the dispute during a session devoted to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appeal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>appeal</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法官在专门处理上诉的庭审中听取了调解争端的请求。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A final session may mediate a settlement if both parties accept the plea to suspend the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appeal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>appeal</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果双方接受暂停上诉的请求，最后一次会议可能促成调解和解。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">file an appeal</span> · 提起上诉</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=settlement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">settlement</a></span> · 和解</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compromise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compromise</a></span> · 妥协</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-046">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-046" class="vocabulary-word-title">contradict<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-046" aria-label="定位 contradict">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-046" data-mark-label="contradict" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="contradict：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="contradict：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contradict&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 contradict" title="在有道查询 contradict">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-046</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.反驳；与......矛盾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The controversy grew when experts used new data to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contradict&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contradict</strong></a> claims and persuade the public.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">专家用新数据反驳主张并说服公众时，争议扩大了。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To resolve the controversy, we must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contradict&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contradict</strong></a> false allegations and persuade both sides to examine the evidence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为解决争议，我们必须反驳虚假指控，并说服双方审查证据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An emotional speech may persuade supporters, but facts that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contradict&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contradict</strong></a> it can prolong the controversy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">煽情演讲也许能说服支持者，但与之矛盾的事实可能延长争议。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">contradict a claim</span> · 反驳主张</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evidence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">evidence</a></span> · 证据</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allegation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">allegation</a></span> · 指控</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-047">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-047" class="vocabulary-word-title">persuade<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-047" aria-label="定位 persuade">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-047" data-mark-label="persuade" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="persuade：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="persuade：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=persuade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 persuade" title="在有道查询 persuade">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-047</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 说服；使相信</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The controversy grew when experts used new data to contradict claims and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=persuade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>persuade</strong></a> the public.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">专家用新数据反驳主张并说服公众时，争议扩大了。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To resolve the controversy, we must contradict false allegations and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=persuade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>persuade</strong></a> both sides to examine the evidence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为解决争议，我们必须反驳虚假指控，并说服双方审查证据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An emotional speech may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=persuade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>persuade</strong></a> supporters, but facts that contradict it can prolong the controversy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">煽情演讲也许能说服支持者，但与之矛盾的事实可能延长争议。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">persuade an audience</span> · 说服听众</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evidence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">evidence</a></span> · 证据</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allegation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">allegation</a></span> · 指控</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-048">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-048" class="vocabulary-word-title">controversy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-048" aria-label="定位 controversy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-048" data-mark-label="controversy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="controversy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="controversy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=controversy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 controversy" title="在有道查询 controversy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-048</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 争议；辩论</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=controversy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>controversy</strong></a> grew when experts used new data to contradict claims and persuade the public.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">专家用新数据反驳主张并说服公众时，争议扩大了。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To resolve the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=controversy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>controversy</strong></a>, we must contradict false allegations and persuade both sides to examine the evidence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为解决争议，我们必须反驳虚假指控，并说服双方审查证据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An emotional speech may persuade supporters, but facts that contradict it can prolong the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=controversy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>controversy</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">煽情演讲也许能说服支持者，但与之矛盾的事实可能延长争议。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a public controversy</span> · 公共争议</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evidence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">evidence</a></span> · 证据</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allegation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">allegation</a></span> · 指控</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 17.05｜如何陈述、调查和辩护 {#group-17-05}

**串联方式：** 先辨低声说与沉默，再通过调查、证人和理由说明真假及合理性。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-049">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-049" class="vocabulary-word-title">whisper<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-049" aria-label="定位 whisper">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-049" data-mark-label="whisper" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="whisper：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="whisper：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whisper&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 whisper" title="在有道查询 whisper">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-049</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 低声说；私下谈论</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whisper&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whisper</strong></a> from the gallery became a murmur, prompting the clerk to mutter a warning.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旁听席的一声耳语变成低语声，令书记员低声发出警告。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Please do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whisper&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whisper</strong></a> or mutter while the witness speaks, even if you hear a murmur of disagreement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">证人发言时，即使听到低声异议，也请不要耳语或嘟哝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The actor changed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whisper&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whisper</strong></a> into a murmur before beginning to mutter in frustration.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演员先从耳语转为低语，随后沮丧地嘟哝起来。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">whisper quietly</span> · 低声耳语</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tone%20of%20voice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tone of voice</a></span> · 语气</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=audience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">audience</a></span> · 听众</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-050">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-050" class="vocabulary-word-title">mutter<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-050" aria-label="定位 mutter">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-050" data-mark-label="mutter" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mutter：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mutter：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mutter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mutter" title="在有道查询 mutter">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-050</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.轻声低语；小声抱怨</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A whisper from the gallery became a murmur, prompting the clerk to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mutter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mutter</strong></a> a warning.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旁听席的一声耳语变成低语声，令书记员低声发出警告。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Please do not whisper or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mutter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mutter</strong></a> while the witness speaks, even if you hear a murmur of disagreement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">证人发言时，即使听到低声异议，也请不要耳语或嘟哝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The actor changed a whisper into a murmur before beginning to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mutter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mutter</strong></a> in frustration.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演员先从耳语转为低语，随后沮丧地嘟哝起来。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">mutter a complaint</span> · 小声抱怨</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tone%20of%20voice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tone of voice</a></span> · 语气</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=audience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">audience</a></span> · 听众</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-051">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-051" class="vocabulary-word-title">murmur<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-051" aria-label="定位 murmur">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-051" data-mark-label="murmur" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="murmur：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="murmur：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=murmur&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 murmur" title="在有道查询 murmur">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-051</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 低声说；n. 咕哝，低语</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A whisper from the gallery became a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=murmur&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>murmur</strong></a>, prompting the clerk to mutter a warning.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旁听席的一声耳语变成低语声，令书记员低声发出警告。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Please do not whisper or mutter while the witness speaks, even if you hear a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=murmur&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>murmur</strong></a> of disagreement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">证人发言时，即使听到低声异议，也请不要耳语或嘟哝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The actor changed a whisper into a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=murmur&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>murmur</strong></a> before beginning to mutter in frustration.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演员先从耳语转为低语，随后沮丧地嘟哝起来。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a murmur of agreement</span> · 低声赞同</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tone%20of%20voice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tone of voice</a></span> · 语气</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=audience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">audience</a></span> · 听众</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-052">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-052" class="vocabulary-word-title">silent<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-052" aria-label="定位 silent">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-052" data-mark-label="silent" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="silent：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="silent：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=silent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 silent" title="在有道查询 silent">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-052</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 沉默的；寂静无声的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The room remained <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=silent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>silent</strong></a> because every microphone was on mute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">由于每个麦克风都处于静音状态，房间里一片寂静。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Please keep your phone <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=silent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>silent</strong></a> and your microphone on mute during the hearing.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">听证时，请将手机调为静音，并关闭麦克风。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=silent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>silent</strong></a> recording showed the witness speaking with the audio on mute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这段无声录像显示证人在讲话，但音频被静音了。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">remain silent</span> · 保持沉默</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=microphone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">microphone</a></span> · 麦克风</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recording&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">recording</a></span> · 录音</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-053">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-053" class="vocabulary-word-title">mute<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-053" aria-label="定位 mute">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-053" data-mark-label="mute" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mute：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mute：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mute" title="在有道查询 mute">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-053</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 缄默的；哑的；无声的；n. 哑巴</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The room remained silent because every microphone was on <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mute</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">由于每个麦克风都处于静音状态，房间里一片寂静。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Please keep your phone silent and your microphone on <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mute</strong></a> during the hearing.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">听证时，请将手机调为静音，并关闭麦克风。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The silent recording showed the witness speaking with the audio on <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mute</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这段无声录像显示证人在讲话，但音频被静音了。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">on mute</span> · 处于静音状态</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=microphone&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">microphone</a></span> · 麦克风</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recording&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">recording</a></span> · 录音</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-054">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-054" class="vocabulary-word-title">investigate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-054" aria-label="定位 investigate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-054" data-mark-label="investigate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="investigate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="investigate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investigate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 investigate" title="在有道查询 investigate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-054</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 调查</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Police must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investigate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>investigate</strong></a> the witness account before using it to justify an arrest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警方必须调查证人的陈述，才能据此证明逮捕正当。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A witness asked the agency to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investigate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>investigate</strong></a> whether the evidence could justify its decision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位证人请该机构调查证据是否足以证明其决定正当。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investigate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>investigate</strong></a> the complaint and ask the witness to justify the allegation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员将调查投诉，并要求证人说明指控依据。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">investigate a complaint</span> · 调查投诉</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=testimony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">testimony</a></span> · 证词</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investigation</a></span> · 调查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-055">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-055" class="vocabulary-word-title">witness<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-055" aria-label="定位 witness">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-055" data-mark-label="witness" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="witness：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="witness：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=witness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 witness" title="在有道查询 witness">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-055</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 目击者，见证人；v. 目击</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Police must investigate the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=witness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>witness</strong></a> account before using it to justify an arrest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警方必须调查证人的陈述，才能据此证明逮捕正当。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=witness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>witness</strong></a> asked the agency to investigate whether the evidence could justify its decision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位证人请该机构调查证据是否足以证明其决定正当。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials will investigate the complaint and ask the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=witness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>witness</strong></a> to justify the allegation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员将调查投诉，并要求证人说明指控依据。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a reliable witness</span> · 可靠证人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=testimony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">testimony</a></span> · 证词</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investigation</a></span> · 调查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-056">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-056" class="vocabulary-word-title">justify<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-056" aria-label="定位 justify">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-056" data-mark-label="justify" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="justify：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="justify：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=justify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 justify" title="在有道查询 justify">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-056</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 为......辩护，证明……正当</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Police must investigate the witness account before using it to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=justify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>justify</strong></a> an arrest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警方必须调查证人的陈述，才能据此证明逮捕正当。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A witness asked the agency to investigate whether the evidence could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=justify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>justify</strong></a> its decision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位证人请该机构调查证据是否足以证明其决定正当。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials will investigate the complaint and ask the witness to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=justify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>justify</strong></a> the allegation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员将调查投诉，并要求证人说明指控依据。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">justify a decision</span> · 证明决定正当</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=testimony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">testimony</a></span> · 证词</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=investigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">investigation</a></span> · 调查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-057">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-057" class="vocabulary-word-title">reasonable<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-057" aria-label="定位 reasonable">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-057" data-mark-label="reasonable" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reasonable：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reasonable：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reasonable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reasonable" title="在有道查询 reasonable">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-057</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 合理的；理智的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reasonable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reasonable</strong></a> investigation needs suitable methods to distinguish a false claim from a genuine one.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">合理调查需要合适方法来区分虚假与真实主张。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">It is <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reasonable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reasonable</strong></a> to seek suitable evidence before declaring a statement false.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在认定陈述虚假之前寻找合适证据是合理的。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The adviser rejected the false accusation and suggested a suitable, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reasonable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reasonable</strong></a> response.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">顾问否定了虚假指控，并建议作出合适而合理的回应。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a reasonable explanation</span> · 合理解释</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verification</a></span> · 核实</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accuracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accuracy</a></span> · 准确性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-058">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-058" class="vocabulary-word-title">suitable<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-058" aria-label="定位 suitable">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-058" data-mark-label="suitable" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="suitable：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="suitable：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suitable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 suitable" title="在有道查询 suitable">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-058</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 合适的，适宜的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A reasonable investigation needs <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suitable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suitable</strong></a> methods to distinguish a false claim from a genuine one.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">合理调查需要合适方法来区分虚假与真实主张。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">It is reasonable to seek <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suitable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suitable</strong></a> evidence before declaring a statement false.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在认定陈述虚假之前寻找合适证据是合理的。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The adviser rejected the false accusation and suggested a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suitable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suitable</strong></a>, reasonable response.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">顾问否定了虚假指控，并建议作出合适而合理的回应。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">suitable evidence</span> · 合适的证据</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verification</a></span> · 核实</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accuracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accuracy</a></span> · 准确性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-059">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-059" class="vocabulary-word-title">FALSE<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-059" aria-label="定位 FALSE">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-059" data-mark-label="FALSE" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="FALSE：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="FALSE：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=FALSE&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 FALSE" title="在有道查询 FALSE">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-059</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 错误的；虚假的；词表大写，普通行文用 false</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A reasonable investigation needs suitable methods to distinguish a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=FALSE&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>false</strong></a> claim from a genuine one.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">合理调查需要合适方法来区分虚假与真实主张。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">It is reasonable to seek suitable evidence before declaring a statement <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=FALSE&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>false</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在认定陈述虚假之前寻找合适证据是合理的。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The adviser rejected the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=FALSE&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>false</strong></a> accusation and suggested a suitable, reasonable response.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">顾问否定了虚假指控，并建议作出合适而合理的回应。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a false statement</span> · 虚假陈述</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verification</a></span> · 核实</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accuracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accuracy</a></span> · 准确性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 17.06｜从审判到羁押与监管 {#group-17-06}

**串联方式：** 由法官、陪审团和审判进入嫌疑人、判决、逮捕与监禁，再看监督和限制。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-060">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-060" class="vocabulary-word-title">judge<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-060" aria-label="定位 judge">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-060" data-mark-label="judge" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="judge：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="judge：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=judge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 judge" title="在有道查询 judge">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-060</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 法官；裁判员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the trial, the jury considered the suspect's guilt before the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=judge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>judge</strong></a> imposed a sentence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">庭审中，陪审团先认定嫌疑人是否有罪，随后法官作出判决。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=judge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>judge</strong></a> reminded the jury that a suspect must receive a fair trial before any sentence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法官提醒陪审团，嫌疑人在任何判刑之前都必须接受公正审判。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A sentence followed only after the jury found the suspect guilty at a trial overseen by the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=judge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>judge</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">只有在法官主持的审判中，陪审团认定嫌疑人有罪后，才会作出刑罚判决。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a trial judge</span> · 审判法官</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verdict&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verdict</a></span> · 裁决</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=presumption%20of%20innocence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">presumption of innocence</a></span> · 无罪推定</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-061">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-061" class="vocabulary-word-title">jury<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-061" aria-label="定位 jury">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-061" data-mark-label="jury" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="jury：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="jury：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jury&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 jury" title="在有道查询 jury">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-061</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.陪审团；(比赛的)评判委员会</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the trial, the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jury&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jury</strong></a> considered the suspect's guilt before the judge imposed a sentence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">庭审中，陪审团先认定嫌疑人是否有罪，随后法官作出判决。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The judge reminded the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jury&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jury</strong></a> that a suspect must receive a fair trial before any sentence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法官提醒陪审团，嫌疑人在任何判刑之前都必须接受公正审判。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A sentence followed only after the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jury&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jury</strong></a> found the suspect guilty at a trial overseen by the judge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">只有在法官主持的审判中，陪审团认定嫌疑人有罪后，才会作出刑罚判决。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a jury verdict</span> · 陪审团裁决</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verdict&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verdict</a></span> · 裁决</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=presumption%20of%20innocence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">presumption of innocence</a></span> · 无罪推定</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-062">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-062" class="vocabulary-word-title">trial<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-062" aria-label="定位 trial">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-062" data-mark-label="trial" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="trial：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="trial：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 trial" title="在有道查询 trial">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-062</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 试验；审判</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trial</strong></a>, the jury considered the suspect's guilt before the judge imposed a sentence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">庭审中，陪审团先认定嫌疑人是否有罪，随后法官作出判决。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The judge reminded the jury that a suspect must receive a fair <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trial</strong></a> before any sentence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法官提醒陪审团，嫌疑人在任何判刑之前都必须接受公正审判。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A sentence followed only after the jury found the suspect guilty at a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trial</strong></a> overseen by the judge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">只有在法官主持的审判中，陪审团认定嫌疑人有罪后，才会作出刑罚判决。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a fair trial</span> · 公正审判</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verdict&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verdict</a></span> · 裁决</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=presumption%20of%20innocence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">presumption of innocence</a></span> · 无罪推定</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-063">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-063" class="vocabulary-word-title">suspect<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-063" aria-label="定位 suspect">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-063" data-mark-label="suspect" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="suspect：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="suspect：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suspect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 suspect" title="在有道查询 suspect">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-063</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 嫌疑犯；v. 怀疑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the trial, the jury considered the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suspect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suspect</strong></a>'s guilt before the judge imposed a sentence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">庭审中，陪审团先认定嫌疑人是否有罪，随后法官作出判决。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The judge reminded the jury that a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suspect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suspect</strong></a> must receive a fair trial before any sentence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法官提醒陪审团，嫌疑人在任何判刑之前都必须接受公正审判。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A sentence followed only after the jury found the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suspect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suspect</strong></a> guilty at a trial overseen by the judge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">只有在法官主持的审判中，陪审团认定嫌疑人有罪后，才会作出刑罚判决。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a prime suspect</span> · 主要嫌疑人</p>
