<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verdict&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verdict</a></span> · 裁决</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=presumption%20of%20innocence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">presumption of innocence</a></span> · 无罪推定</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-064">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-064" class="vocabulary-word-title">sentence<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-064" aria-label="定位 sentence">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-064" data-mark-label="sentence" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sentence：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sentence：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sentence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sentence" title="在有道查询 sentence">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-064</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 判决；v. 宣判</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the trial, the jury considered the suspect's guilt before the judge imposed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sentence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sentence</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">庭审中，陪审团先认定嫌疑人是否有罪，随后法官作出判决。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The judge reminded the jury that a suspect must receive a fair trial before any <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sentence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sentence</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法官提醒陪审团，嫌疑人在任何判刑之前都必须接受公正审判。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sentence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sentence</strong></a> followed only after the jury found the suspect guilty at a trial overseen by the judge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">只有在法官主持的审判中，陪审团认定嫌疑人有罪后，才会作出刑罚判决。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a prison sentence</span> · 监禁判决</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verdict&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verdict</a></span> · 裁决</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=presumption%20of%20innocence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">presumption of innocence</a></span> · 无罪推定</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-065">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-065" class="vocabulary-word-title">arrest<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-065" aria-label="定位 arrest">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-065" data-mark-label="arrest" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="arrest：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="arrest：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 arrest" title="在有道查询 arrest">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-065</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 逮捕；吸引(注意)；n. 逮捕</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arrest</strong></a> does not automatically permit officials to confine someone in jail or prison indefinitely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">逮捕并不自动允许官员无限期将某人关在看守所或监狱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report followed an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arrest</strong></a> through detention in jail to a court order to confine the offender in prison.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告追踪了从逮捕、看守所拘留，到法院下令将罪犯关入监狱的过程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arrest</strong></a> and confine a person in jail, officers must explain the grounds for detention and possible prison proceedings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">逮捕并将某人关入看守所前，警员必须说明拘留依据及可能的监禁程序。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">make an arrest</span> · 实施逮捕</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">detention</a></span> · 拘留</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=due%20process&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">due process</a></span> · 正当程序</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-066">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-066" class="vocabulary-word-title">jail<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-066" aria-label="定位 jail">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-066" data-mark-label="jail" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="jail：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="jail：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 jail" title="在有道查询 jail">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-066</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.监狱；v.监禁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An arrest does not automatically permit officials to confine someone in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jail</strong></a> or prison indefinitely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">逮捕并不自动允许官员无限期将某人关在看守所或监狱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report followed an arrest through detention in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jail</strong></a> to a court order to confine the offender in prison.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告追踪了从逮捕、看守所拘留，到法院下令将罪犯关入监狱的过程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they arrest and confine a person in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jail</strong></a>, officers must explain the grounds for detention and possible prison proceedings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">逮捕并将某人关入看守所前，警员必须说明拘留依据及可能的监禁程序。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">send to jail</span> · 送进监狱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">detention</a></span> · 拘留</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=due%20process&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">due process</a></span> · 正当程序</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-067">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-067" class="vocabulary-word-title">prison<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-067" aria-label="定位 prison">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-067" data-mark-label="prison" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="prison：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="prison：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prison&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 prison" title="在有道查询 prison">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-067</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.监狱；监禁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An arrest does not automatically permit officials to confine someone in jail or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prison&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prison</strong></a> indefinitely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">逮捕并不自动允许官员无限期将某人关在看守所或监狱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report followed an arrest through detention in jail to a court order to confine the offender in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prison&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prison</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告追踪了从逮捕、看守所拘留，到法院下令将罪犯关入监狱的过程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they arrest and confine a person in jail, officers must explain the grounds for detention and possible <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prison&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prison</strong></a> proceedings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">逮捕并将某人关入看守所前，警员必须说明拘留依据及可能的监禁程序。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a prison cell</span> · 牢房</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">detention</a></span> · 拘留</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=due%20process&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">due process</a></span> · 正当程序</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-068">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-068" class="vocabulary-word-title">confine<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-068" aria-label="定位 confine">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-068" data-mark-label="confine" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="confine：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="confine：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=confine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 confine" title="在有道查询 confine">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-068</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. [~s] 边界，范围；v.限制；监禁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An arrest does not automatically permit officials to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=confine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>confine</strong></a> someone in jail or prison indefinitely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">逮捕并不自动允许官员无限期将某人关在看守所或监狱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report followed an arrest through detention in jail to a court order to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=confine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>confine</strong></a> the offender in prison.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告追踪了从逮捕、看守所拘留，到法院下令将罪犯关入监狱的过程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they arrest and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=confine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>confine</strong></a> a person in jail, officers must explain the grounds for detention and possible prison proceedings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">逮捕并将某人关入看守所前，警员必须说明拘留依据及可能的监禁程序。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">confine a prisoner</span> · 关押囚犯</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">detention</a></span> · 拘留</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=due%20process&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">due process</a></span> · 正当程序</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-069">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-069" class="vocabulary-word-title">oversee<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-069" aria-label="定位 oversee">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-069" data-mark-label="oversee" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="oversee：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="oversee：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 oversee" title="在有道查询 oversee">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-069</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 监督，监管</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Senior officers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oversee</strong></a> security, supervise guards, restrict access and restrain violent visitors.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">高级警官监管安全、监督警卫、限制进入并制止暴力访客。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspectors who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oversee</strong></a> the centre must supervise staff who restrict movement or restrain residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">监管中心的检查员必须监督那些限制行动或约束住民的工作人员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oversee</strong></a> the event safely, organisers will supervise volunteers, restrict entry and restrain anyone attempting violence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为安全监管活动，组织者将监督志愿者、限制入场，并制止任何企图施暴的人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">oversee a project</span> · 监督项目</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oversight</a></span> · 监督</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20safety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public safety</a></span> · 公共安全</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-070">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-070" class="vocabulary-word-title">supervise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-070" aria-label="定位 supervise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-070" data-mark-label="supervise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="supervise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="supervise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=supervise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 supervise" title="在有道查询 supervise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-070</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.监督，管理，指导</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Senior officers oversee security, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=supervise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>supervise</strong></a> guards, restrict access and restrain violent visitors.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">高级警官监管安全、监督警卫、限制进入并制止暴力访客。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspectors who oversee the centre must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=supervise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>supervise</strong></a> staff who restrict movement or restrain residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">监管中心的检查员必须监督那些限制行动或约束住民的工作人员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To oversee the event safely, organisers will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=supervise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>supervise</strong></a> volunteers, restrict entry and restrain anyone attempting violence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为安全监管活动，组织者将监督志愿者、限制入场，并制止任何企图施暴的人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">supervise staff</span> · 监督员工</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oversight</a></span> · 监督</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20safety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public safety</a></span> · 公共安全</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-071">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-071" class="vocabulary-word-title">restrict<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-071" aria-label="定位 restrict">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-071" data-mark-label="restrict" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="restrict：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="restrict：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restrict&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 restrict" title="在有道查询 restrict">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-071</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 限制，限定；约束</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Senior officers oversee security, supervise guards, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restrict&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>restrict</strong></a> access and restrain violent visitors.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">高级警官监管安全、监督警卫、限制进入并制止暴力访客。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspectors who oversee the centre must supervise staff who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restrict&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>restrict</strong></a> movement or restrain residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">监管中心的检查员必须监督那些限制行动或约束住民的工作人员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To oversee the event safely, organisers will supervise volunteers, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restrict&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>restrict</strong></a> entry and restrain anyone attempting violence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为安全监管活动，组织者将监督志愿者、限制入场，并制止任何企图施暴的人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">restrict access</span> · 限制进入</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oversight</a></span> · 监督</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20safety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public safety</a></span> · 公共安全</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-072">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-072" class="vocabulary-word-title">restrain<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-072" aria-label="定位 restrain">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-072" data-mark-label="restrain" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="restrain：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="restrain：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restrain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 restrain" title="在有道查询 restrain">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-072</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 抑制；制止</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Senior officers oversee security, supervise guards, restrict access and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restrain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>restrain</strong></a> violent visitors.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">高级警官监管安全、监督警卫、限制进入并制止暴力访客。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspectors who oversee the centre must supervise staff who restrict movement or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restrain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>restrain</strong></a> residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">监管中心的检查员必须监督那些限制行动或约束住民的工作人员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To oversee the event safely, organisers will supervise volunteers, restrict entry and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restrain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>restrain</strong></a> anyone attempting violence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为安全监管活动，组织者将监督志愿者、限制入场，并制止任何企图施暴的人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">restrain violent behaviour</span> · 制止暴力行为</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oversight</a></span> · 监督</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20safety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public safety</a></span> · 公共安全</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 17.07｜识别违法行为及相关社会议题 {#group-17-07}

**串联方式：** 从犯罪、贿赂、抢劫、盗窃到谋杀；suicide 是相关社会与法律语境词，不将其一概归为犯罪。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-073">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-073" class="vocabulary-word-title">commit<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-073" aria-label="定位 commit">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-073" data-mark-label="commit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="commit：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="commit：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 commit" title="在有道查询 commit">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-073</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 犯(罪)，做(坏事)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A vice is not always a crime, and beliefs about sin do not determine whether someone can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commit</strong></a> an offence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">恶习不总是犯罪，对罪孽的看法也不决定一个人的行为是否违法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The essay asks why people <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commit</strong></a> a crime and how society distinguishes crime from sin or vice.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">文章探讨人们为何犯罪，以及社会如何区分犯罪、罪孽与恶习。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Religious leaders may condemn a vice as sin even when people who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commit</strong></a> it do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commit</strong></a> a crime.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">宗教领袖可能把某种恶习谴责为罪孽，即使犯此行为的人并未犯罪。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">commit a crime</span> · 犯罪</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=morality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">morality</a></span> · 道德</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">offence</a></span> · 违法行为</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-074">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-074" class="vocabulary-word-title">sin<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-074" aria-label="定位 sin">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-074" data-mark-label="sin" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sin：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sin：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sin" title="在有道查询 sin">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-074</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 罪孽；v. 犯戒律</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A vice is not always a crime, and beliefs about <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sin</strong></a> do not determine whether someone can commit an offence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">恶习不总是犯罪，对罪孽的看法也不决定一个人的行为是否违法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The essay asks why people commit a crime and how society distinguishes crime from <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sin</strong></a> or vice.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">文章探讨人们为何犯罪，以及社会如何区分犯罪、罪孽与恶习。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Religious leaders may condemn a vice as <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sin</strong></a> even when people who commit it do not commit a crime.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">宗教领袖可能把某种恶习谴责为罪孽，即使犯此行为的人并未犯罪。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a mortal sin</span> · 严重罪孽</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=morality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">morality</a></span> · 道德</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">offence</a></span> · 违法行为</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The essay asks why people commit a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crime&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crime</strong></a> and how society distinguishes <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crime&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>crime</strong></a> from sin or vice.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">文章探讨人们为何犯罪，以及社会如何区分犯罪、罪孽与恶习。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The essay asks why people commit a crime and how society distinguishes crime from sin or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vice</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">文章探讨人们为何犯罪，以及社会如何区分犯罪、罪孽与恶习。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Religious leaders may condemn a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vice</strong></a> as sin even when people who commit it do not commit a crime.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">宗教领袖可能把某种恶习谴责为罪孽，即使犯此行为的人并未犯罪。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-077">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-077" class="vocabulary-word-title">bribe<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-077" aria-label="定位 bribe">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-077" data-mark-label="bribe" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bribe：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bribe：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bribe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bribe" title="在有道查询 bribe">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-077</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 贿赂；v. 向......行贿</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The thief refused to accept a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bribe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bribe</strong></a> from a pirate who wanted help to rob a warehouse and steal supplies.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">窃贼拒绝了海盗的贿赂；海盗想让他帮助抢劫仓库、窃取物资。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A pirate may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bribe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bribe</strong></a> a guard, a thief may steal cargo, and armed raiders may rob the crew.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">海盗可能贿赂守卫，窃贼可能偷货，而武装袭击者可能抢劫船员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel follows a thief who must choose whether to take a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bribe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bribe</strong></a>, join a pirate and rob ships to steal gold.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说描写一个窃贼，面对是否收受贿赂、加入海盗并抢船偷金的抉择。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">accept a bribe</span> · 受贿</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=theft&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">theft</a></span> · 盗窃</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=robbery&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">robbery</a></span> · 抢劫</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-078">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-078" class="vocabulary-word-title">rob<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-078" aria-label="定位 rob">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-078" data-mark-label="rob" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rob：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rob：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rob&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rob" title="在有道查询 rob">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-078</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.抢劫；夺走</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The thief refused to accept a bribe from a pirate who wanted help to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rob&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rob</strong></a> a warehouse and steal supplies.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">窃贼拒绝了海盗的贿赂；海盗想让他帮助抢劫仓库、窃取物资。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A pirate may bribe a guard, a thief may steal cargo, and armed raiders may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rob&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rob</strong></a> the crew.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">海盗可能贿赂守卫，窃贼可能偷货，而武装袭击者可能抢劫船员。</p></li>

### 词群 17.08｜欺骗、坦白与遵从 {#group-17-08}

**串联方式：** 先看欺骗、诈骗、谎言和圈套，再讨论污名、坦白、和解、服从与屈从。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-084">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-084" class="vocabulary-word-title">deceive<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-084" aria-label="定位 deceive">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-084" data-mark-label="deceive" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="deceive：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="deceive：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deceive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 deceive" title="在有道查询 deceive">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-084</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.欺骗；蒙蔽，误导</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A liar may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deceive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deceive</strong></a> investors, cheat customers and commit fraud.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">说谎者可能欺骗投资者、坑骗顾客并实施诈骗。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The inquiry examined how the liar used fraud to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deceive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deceive</strong></a> lenders and cheat elderly clients.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查考察了这位说谎者如何通过诈骗欺骗贷款方并坑害老年客户。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Calling someone a liar does not prove fraud; investigators must show an attempt to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deceive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deceive</strong></a> or cheat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">称某人为说谎者并不能证明诈骗；调查人员必须证实其欺骗或坑害的企图。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">deceive investors</span> · 欺骗投资者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dishonesty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dishonesty</a></span> · 不诚实</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">scam</a></span> · 骗局</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-085">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-085" class="vocabulary-word-title">cheat<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-085" aria-label="定位 cheat">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-085" data-mark-label="cheat" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="cheat：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="cheat：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cheat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 cheat" title="在有道查询 cheat">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-085</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 欺骗；作弊；n. 作弊者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A liar may deceive investors, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cheat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cheat</strong></a> customers and commit fraud.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">说谎者可能欺骗投资者、坑骗顾客并实施诈骗。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The inquiry examined how the liar used fraud to deceive lenders and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cheat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cheat</strong></a> elderly clients.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查考察了这位说谎者如何通过诈骗欺骗贷款方并坑害老年客户。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Calling someone a liar does not prove fraud; investigators must show an attempt to deceive or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cheat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cheat</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">称某人为说谎者并不能证明诈骗；调查人员必须证实其欺骗或坑害的企图。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">cheat in an exam</span> · 考试作弊</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dishonesty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dishonesty</a></span> · 不诚实</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">scam</a></span> · 骗局</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 骗子；赝品；欺诈</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A liar may deceive investors, cheat customers and commit <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fraud&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fraud</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">说谎者可能欺骗投资者、坑骗顾客并实施诈骗。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The inquiry examined how the liar used <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fraud&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fraud</strong></a> to deceive lenders and cheat elderly clients.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查考察了这位说谎者如何通过诈骗欺骗贷款方并坑害老年客户。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Calling someone a liar does not prove <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fraud&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fraud</strong></a>; investigators must show an attempt to deceive or cheat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">称某人为说谎者并不能证明诈骗；调查人员必须证实其欺骗或坑害的企图。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">financial fraud</span> · 金融诈骗</p>

</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=liar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>liar</strong></a> may deceive investors, cheat customers and commit fraud.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">说谎者可能欺骗投资者、坑骗顾客并实施诈骗。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The inquiry examined how the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=liar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>liar</strong></a> used fraud to deceive lenders and cheat elderly clients.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查考察了这位说谎者如何通过诈骗欺骗贷款方并坑害老年客户。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Calling someone a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=liar&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>liar</strong></a> does not prove fraud; investigators must show an attempt to deceive or cheat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">称某人为说谎者并不能证明诈骗；调查人员必须证实其欺骗或坑害的企图。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-088">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-088" class="vocabulary-word-title">trap<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-088" aria-label="定位 trap">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-088" data-mark-label="trap" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="trap：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="trap：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 trap" title="在有道查询 trap">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-088</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 陷阱；诡计 ，诱骗</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scammers pretend to be officials and use a trick to fool people into a financial <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trap</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">骗子假扮官员，用诡计愚弄人们，使其落入金融圈套。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not pretend that this trick can fool an experienced investigator into your <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trap</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">别装作这招能骗得有经验的调查员落入你的圈套。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The fraudsters' trick was to pretend to offer help, fool the victim and draw her into a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trap</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">诈骗者的诡计是假装提供帮助，欺骗受害者，让她落入圈套。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">fall into a trap</span> · 落入圈套</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=impersonation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">impersonation</a></span> · 冒充</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=victim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">victim</a></span> · 受害者</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scammers pretend to be officials and use a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trick&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trick</strong></a> to fool people into a financial trap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">骗子假扮官员，用诡计愚弄人们，使其落入金融圈套。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not pretend that this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trick&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trick</strong></a> can fool an experienced investigator into your trap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">别装作这招能骗得有经验的调查员落入你的圈套。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The fraudsters' <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trick&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trick</strong></a> was to pretend to offer help, fool the victim and draw her into a trap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">诈骗者的诡计是假装提供帮助，欺骗受害者，让她落入圈套。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">play a trick</span> · 耍花招</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=impersonation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">impersonation</a></span> · 冒充</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=victim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">victim</a></span> · 受害者</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scammers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pretend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pretend</strong></a> to be officials and use a trick to fool people into a financial trap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">骗子假扮官员，用诡计愚弄人们，使其落入金融圈套。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pretend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pretend</strong></a> that this trick can fool an experienced investigator into your trap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">别装作这招能骗得有经验的调查员落入你的圈套。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The fraudsters' trick was to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pretend&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pretend</strong></a> to offer help, fool the victim and draw her into a trap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">诈骗者的诡计是假装提供帮助，欺骗受害者，让她落入圈套。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">pretend to agree</span> · 假装同意</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-092">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-092" class="vocabulary-word-title">stigma<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-092" aria-label="定位 stigma">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-092" data-mark-label="stigma" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="stigma：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="stigma：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stigma&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 stigma" title="在有道查询 stigma">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-092</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 耻辱， 污名</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The programme helps former offenders confess mistakes, confront <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stigma&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stigma</strong></a>, conform to lawful standards and reconcile with families.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该项目帮助有前科者承认错误、面对污名、遵守合法标准并与家人和解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fear of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stigma&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stigma</strong></a> may stop people from trying to confess, conform to new rules or reconcile with former friends.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">对污名的恐惧可能阻止人们坦白、遵守新规则或与旧友和解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Counsellors encourage participants to confess honestly and reconcile their values with social expectations without forcing them to conform through <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stigma&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stigma</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">咨询师鼓励参与者诚实坦白、调和价值观与社会期望，而非用污名迫使他们顺从。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">social stigma</span> · 社会污名</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reintegration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reintegration</a></span> · 重新融入社会</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=forgiveness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">forgiveness</a></span> · 宽恕</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fear of stigma may stop people from trying to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=confess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>confess</strong></a>, conform to new rules or reconcile with former friends.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">对污名的恐惧可能阻止人们坦白、遵守新规则或与旧友和解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Counsellors encourage participants to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=confess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>confess</strong></a> honestly and reconcile their values with social expectations without forcing them to conform through stigma.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">咨询师鼓励参与者诚实坦白、调和价值观与社会期望，而非用污名迫使他们顺从。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fear of stigma may stop people from trying to confess, conform to new rules or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reconcile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reconcile</strong></a> with former friends.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">对污名的恐惧可能阻止人们坦白、遵守新规则或与旧友和解。</p></li>

</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-096">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-096" class="vocabulary-word-title">obey<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-096" aria-label="定位 obey">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-096" data-mark-label="obey" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="obey：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="obey：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obey&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 obey" title="在有道查询 obey">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-096</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 服从， 顺从</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obey&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>obey</strong></a> the law and refuse to succumb to political pressure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员必须遵守法律，并拒绝屈服于政治压力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Citizens should not have to succumb to intimidation simply to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obey&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>obey</strong></a> an unjust order.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公民不应仅为服从不公命令而被迫屈服于恐吓。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The witness chose to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obey&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>obey</strong></a> the court rather than succumb to threats.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">证人选择服从法院，而非屈服于威胁。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">obey the law</span> · 遵守法律</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compliance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compliance</a></span> · 遵从</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intimidation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intimidation</a></span> · 恐吓</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-097">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-097" class="vocabulary-word-title">succumb<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-097" aria-label="定位 succumb">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-097" data-mark-label="succumb" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="succumb：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="succumb：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=succumb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 succumb" title="在有道查询 succumb">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-097</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 屈服，屈从</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials must obey the law and refuse to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=succumb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>succumb</strong></a> to political pressure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员必须遵守法律，并拒绝屈服于政治压力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Citizens should not have to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=succumb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>succumb</strong></a> to intimidation simply to obey an unjust order.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公民不应仅为服从不公命令而被迫屈服于恐吓。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The witness chose to obey the court rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=succumb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>succumb</strong></a> to threats.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">证人选择服从法院，而非屈服于威胁。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">succumb to pressure</span> · 屈服于压力</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compliance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compliance</a></span> · 遵从</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intimidation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intimidation</a></span> · 恐吓</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 17.09｜处罚、财产与权利 {#group-17-09}

**串联方式：** 先辨罚款，再看抵押、所有权、版权、特权与认可；这些是不同法律关系。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-098">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-098" class="vocabulary-word-title">penalty<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-098" aria-label="定位 penalty">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-098" data-mark-label="penalty" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="penalty：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="penalty：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=penalty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 penalty" title="在有道查询 penalty">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-098</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 惩罚，处罚</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=penalty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>penalty</strong></a> for illegal parking is a fine.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">违法停车的处罚是罚款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A small fine may be an ineffective <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=penalty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>penalty</strong></a> for a wealthy company.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小额罚款对富裕公司可能是无效的处罚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The court replaced the fine with another <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=penalty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>penalty</strong></a> after reviewing the offender's circumstances.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法院审查违法者情况后，用另一种处罚代替罚款。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a financial penalty</span> · 经济处罚</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deterrent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">deterrent</a></span> · 威慑</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enforcement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">enforcement</a></span> · 执法</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-099">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-099" class="vocabulary-word-title">fine<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-099" aria-label="定位 fine">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-099" data-mark-label="fine" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fine：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fine：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fine" title="在有道查询 fine">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-099</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.罚款；adj. 美好的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The penalty for illegal parking is a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fine</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">违法停车的处罚是罚款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A small <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fine</strong></a> may be an ineffective penalty for a wealthy company.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小额罚款对富裕公司可能是无效的处罚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The court replaced the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fine</strong></a> with another penalty after reviewing the offender's circumstances.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法院审查违法者情况后，用另一种处罚代替罚款。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">pay a fine</span> · 缴纳罚款</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deterrent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">deterrent</a></span> · 威慑</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enforcement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">enforcement</a></span> · 执法</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-100">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-100" class="vocabulary-word-title">mortgage<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-100" aria-label="定位 mortgage">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-100" data-mark-label="mortgage" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mortgage：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mortgage：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mortgage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mortgage" title="在有道查询 mortgage">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-100</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 抵押(房屋、土地)借款；n. 房屋抵押贷款</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The seminar covered home ownership, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mortgage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mortgage</strong></a> costs, copyright and the privilege of using protected material.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研讨会讨论了住房所有权、按揭成本、版权，以及使用受保护材料的特殊权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mortgage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mortgage</strong></a> affects property ownership, while copyright determines who has the privilege of reproducing a work.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">按揭影响房产所有权，而版权决定谁有复制作品的特殊权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lawyer distinguished a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mortgage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mortgage</strong></a> from ownership and a copyright licence from an automatic privilege.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">律师区分了按揭与所有权，也区分了版权许可与自动享有的特权。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a mortgage payment</span> · 按揭还款</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intellectual%20property&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intellectual property</a></span> · 知识产权</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=licence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">licence</a></span> · 许可</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-101">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The seminar covered home <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ownership&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ownership</strong></a>, mortgage costs, copyright and the privilege of using protected material.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研讨会讨论了住房所有权、按揭成本、版权，以及使用受保护材料的特殊权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A mortgage affects property <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ownership&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ownership</strong></a>, while copyright determines who has the privilege of reproducing a work.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">按揭影响房产所有权，而版权决定谁有复制作品的特殊权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lawyer distinguished a mortgage from <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ownership&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ownership</strong></a> and a copyright licence from an automatic privilege.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">律师区分了按揭与所有权，也区分了版权许可与自动享有的特权。</p></li>
</ol>
