<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-102">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-102" class="vocabulary-word-title">copyright<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-102" aria-label="定位 copyright">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-102" data-mark-label="copyright" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="copyright：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="copyright：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=copyright&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 copyright" title="在有道查询 copyright">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-102</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.版权</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The seminar covered home ownership, mortgage costs, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=copyright&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>copyright</strong></a> and the privilege of using protected material.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研讨会讨论了住房所有权、按揭成本、版权，以及使用受保护材料的特殊权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A mortgage affects property ownership, while <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=copyright&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>copyright</strong></a> determines who has the privilege of reproducing a work.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">按揭影响房产所有权，而版权决定谁有复制作品的特殊权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lawyer distinguished a mortgage from ownership and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=copyright&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>copyright</strong></a> licence from an automatic privilege.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">律师区分了按揭与所有权，也区分了版权许可与自动享有的特权。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">copyright protection</span> · 版权保护</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intellectual%20property&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intellectual property</a></span> · 知识产权</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=licence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">licence</a></span> · 许可</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-103">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-103" class="vocabulary-word-title">privilege<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-103" aria-label="定位 privilege">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-103" data-mark-label="privilege" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="privilege：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="privilege：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=privilege&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 privilege" title="在有道查询 privilege">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-103</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 特权</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The seminar covered home ownership, mortgage costs, copyright and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=privilege&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>privilege</strong></a> of using protected material.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研讨会讨论了住房所有权、按揭成本、版权，以及使用受保护材料的特殊权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A mortgage affects property ownership, while copyright determines who has the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=privilege&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>privilege</strong></a> of reproducing a work.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">按揭影响房产所有权，而版权决定谁有复制作品的特殊权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lawyer distinguished a mortgage from ownership and a copyright licence from an automatic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=privilege&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>privilege</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">律师区分了按揭与所有权，也区分了版权许可与自动享有的特权。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a special privilege</span> · 特殊权利</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intellectual%20property&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intellectual property</a></span> · 知识产权</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=licence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">licence</a></span> · 许可</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-104">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-104" class="vocabulary-word-title">accredit<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-104" aria-label="定位 accredit">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-104" data-mark-label="accredit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="accredit：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="accredit：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accredit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 accredit" title="在有道查询 accredit">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-104</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V. 把……归于；委任</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The association will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accredit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accredit</strong></a> colleges that meet its standards.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该协会将认证符合其标准的学院。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Independent inspectors assess schools before authorities <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accredit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accredit</strong></a> them.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当局认证学校前，由独立检查员进行评估。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agency can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accredit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accredit</strong></a> training providers after a detailed review.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该机构可在详细审核后认证培训机构。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">accredit an institution</span> · 认证机构</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accreditation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accreditation</a></span> · 认证</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quality%20assurance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">quality assurance</a></span> · 质量保障</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 17.10｜证据线索与程序后果 {#group-17-10}

**串联方式：** 由线索、访问与匿名进入真实性、程序、惯例及结果。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-105">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-105" class="vocabulary-word-title">clue<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-105" aria-label="定位 clue">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-105" data-mark-label="clue" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="clue：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="clue：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 clue" title="在有道查询 clue">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-105</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.线索；提示</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An anonymous source with access to the files supplied a genuine <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>clue</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有权查阅档案的匿名消息来源提供了一条真实线索。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Investigators need access to records to check whether an anonymous <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>clue</strong></a> is genuine.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查人员需查阅档案，核实匿名线索是否真实。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The genuine <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>clue</strong></a> came from an anonymous employee whose access to the archive was limited.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">真实线索来自一名查阅档案权限有限的匿名员工。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a vital clue</span> · 关键线索</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verification</a></span> · 核实</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=source%20protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">source protection</a></span> · 消息来源保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-106">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-106" class="vocabulary-word-title">access<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-106" aria-label="定位 access">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-106" data-mark-label="access" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="access：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="access：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=access&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 access" title="在有道查询 access">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-106</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 通路，入径：查阅(或使用、接近)的机会(或权利)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An anonymous source with <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=access&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>access</strong></a> to the files supplied a genuine clue.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有权查阅档案的匿名消息来源提供了一条真实线索。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Investigators need <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=access&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>access</strong></a> to records to check whether an anonymous clue is genuine.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查人员需查阅档案，核实匿名线索是否真实。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The genuine clue came from an anonymous employee whose <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=access&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>access</strong></a> to the archive was limited.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">真实线索来自一名查阅档案权限有限的匿名员工。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">access to records</span> · 查阅档案的权限</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verification</a></span> · 核实</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=source%20protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">source protection</a></span> · 消息来源保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Investigators need access to records to check whether an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=anonymous&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>anonymous</strong></a> clue is genuine.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查人员需查阅档案，核实匿名线索是否真实。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The genuine clue came from an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=anonymous&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>anonymous</strong></a> employee whose access to the archive was limited.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">真实线索来自一名查阅档案权限有限的匿名员工。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an anonymous source</span> · 匿名消息来源</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-108">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An anonymous source with access to the files supplied a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=genuine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>genuine</strong></a> clue.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有权查阅档案的匿名消息来源提供了一条真实线索。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Investigators need access to records to check whether an anonymous clue is <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=genuine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>genuine</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查人员需查阅档案，核实匿名线索是否真实。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=genuine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>genuine</strong></a> clue came from an anonymous employee whose access to the archive was limited.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">真实线索来自一名查阅档案权限有限的匿名员工。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-109">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-109" class="vocabulary-word-title">procedure<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-109" aria-label="定位 procedure">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-109" data-mark-label="procedure" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="procedure：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="procedure：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=procedure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 procedure" title="在有道查询 procedure">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-109</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 手续；程序</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Ignoring a routine <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=procedure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>procedure</strong></a> can have a serious consequence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">忽视例行程序可能造成严重后果。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=procedure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>procedure</strong></a> makes safety checks routine and reduces the chance of an unintended consequence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新程序将安全检查常规化，降低意外后果的可能性。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A routine review found that the complaint <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=procedure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>procedure</strong></a> had a damaging consequence for vulnerable applicants.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">例行审查发现，投诉程序对弱势申请者产生了有害后果。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">follow a procedure</span> · 遵循程序</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=process&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">process</a></span> · 流程</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=review&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">review</a></span> · 审查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-110">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-110" class="vocabulary-word-title">routine<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-110" aria-label="定位 routine">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-110" data-mark-label="routine" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="routine：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="routine：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=routine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 routine" title="在有道查询 routine">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-110</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 例行公事；需规；adj. 惯例的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Ignoring a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=routine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>routine</strong></a> procedure can have a serious consequence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">忽视例行程序可能造成严重后果。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The new procedure makes safety checks <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=routine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>routine</strong></a> and reduces the chance of an unintended consequence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新程序将安全检查常规化，降低意外后果的可能性。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=routine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>routine</strong></a> review found that the complaint procedure had a damaging consequence for vulnerable applicants.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">例行审查发现，投诉程序对弱势申请者产生了有害后果。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a routine check</span> · 例行检查</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=process&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">process</a></span> · 流程</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=review&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">review</a></span> · 审查</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-111">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-111" class="vocabulary-word-title">consequence<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-111" aria-label="定位 consequence">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-111" data-mark-label="consequence" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="consequence：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="consequence：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consequence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 consequence" title="在有道查询 consequence">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-111</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 后果</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Ignoring a routine procedure can have a serious <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consequence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>consequence</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">忽视例行程序可能造成严重后果。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The new procedure makes safety checks routine and reduces the chance of an unintended <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consequence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>consequence</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新程序将安全检查常规化，降低意外后果的可能性。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A routine review found that the complaint procedure had a damaging <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consequence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>consequence</strong></a> for vulnerable applicants.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">例行审查发现，投诉程序对弱势申请者产生了有害后果。</p></li>

### 词群 17.11｜请求与命令的力度 {#group-17-11}

**串联方式：** 比较要求、请求、需要、请愿、命令和指示，注意主体与语气。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-112">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-112" class="vocabulary-word-title">demand<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-112" aria-label="定位 demand">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-112" data-mark-label="demand" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="demand：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="demand：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 demand" title="在有道查询 demand">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-112</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 要求；需要；n. 要求；需求；需求量</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Residents <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>demand</strong></a> action, submit a request and sign a petition to require a formal response.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民要求行动、提交请求并签署请愿书，以要求正式回应。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The petition makes a clear <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>demand</strong></a>, while officials require evidence before accepting the request.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">请愿书提出明确要求，而官员在接受请求前要求提供证据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A request can become a public <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>demand</strong></a> when residents petition the council to require safer roads.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民请愿要求市政机构改善道路安全时，一项请求可能成为公众诉求。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">demand an explanation</span> · 要求解释</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=civic%20participation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">civic participation</a></span> · 公民参与</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=representation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">representation</a></span> · 代表权</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-113">
<summary><h4 id="word-17-113" class="vocabulary-word-title">request<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-17-113" aria-label="定位 request">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="17-113" data-mark-label="request" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="request：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="request：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=request&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 request" title="在有道查询 request">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">17-113</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.要求，请求；v. 要求，请求</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Residents demand action, submit a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=request&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>request</strong></a> and sign a petition to require a formal response.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民要求行动、提交请求并签署请愿书，以要求正式回应。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The petition makes a clear demand, while officials require evidence before accepting the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=request&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>request</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">请愿书提出明确要求，而官员在接受请求前要求提供证据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=request&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>request</strong></a> can become a public demand when residents petition the council to require safer roads.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民请愿要求市政机构改善道路安全时，一项请求可能成为公众诉求。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">make a request</span> · 提出请求</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="17-114">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Residents demand action, submit a request and sign a petition to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=require&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>require</strong></a> a formal response.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民要求行动、提交请求并签署请愿书，以要求正式回应。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The petition makes a clear demand, while officials <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=require&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>require</strong></a> evidence before accepting the request.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">请愿书提出明确要求，而官员在接受请求前要求提供证据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A request can become a public demand when residents petition the council to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=require&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>require</strong></a> safer roads.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民请愿要求市政机构改善道路安全时，一项请求可能成为公众诉求。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=petition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>petition</strong></a> makes a clear demand, while officials require evidence before accepting the request.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">请愿书提出明确要求，而官员在接受请求前要求提供证据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A request can become a public demand when residents <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=petition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>petition</strong></a> the council to require safer roads.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民请愿要求市政机构改善道路安全时，一项请求可能成为公众诉求。</p></li>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **enforce a regulation**：执行法规。
- **protect ownership rights**：保护所有权；rights 为补充词。
- **follow a legal procedure**：遵循法律程序。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

Laws define rights and obligations and provide procedures for resolving disputes. If someone makes an accusation, investigators look for clues and speak to witnesses. A court considers the case before reaching a judgment. Fair procedures and reliable evidence matter when deciding whether a claim is justified.

**中文理解：** 法律界定权利和义务，并提供解决争议的程序。有人提出指控时，调查者寻找线索并询问证人。法院审理案件后才作判断。判断主张是否有依据时，公正程序和可靠证据都很重要。

### 闭卷回忆｜先尝试，再逐级提示

**任务：** 解释一项规则怎样规定权利义务，以及争议如何通过程序处理。

1. 关掉本章正文，在空白页列出你记得的词群；每群先写 3—5 个英文词或搭配。
2. 从中选 3 个词群，连成 3—5 句英文；用明确关系连接，专业分类和事实不确定时先核对。
3. 卡住时，先只看“本章主线”；还想不起，再看词群标题和中文简释，最后才看英文。
4. 用核对编号找出遗漏词；把“见到认得”与“想表达时能说出”分开记录。
5. 合上提示重新说一遍；下次换一个对象、地点或例子，检查能否迁移。
